- **ai-sdd-brief**: Capture the requirements you compile during an agent session into a planner-ready brief — built incrementally, in place, as you close each decision. The authoring step upstream of ai-sdd-plan / ai-sdd-plan-program: it produces the markdown brief those skills take as input, with the four gated sections they refuse to plan without (goal · scope · acceptance · constraints) already complete. Optimized for the in-session case: the agent that did the research drains what it learned into a living ledger, one closed bucket at a time, instead of reconstructing the format at the end. - **ai-sdd-plan**: Turn a complete feature brief into a runnable plan for a bootstrapped repo — a decision-closed requirements doc and an orchestration graph (slices + depends_on) the engine executes. Requires a real brief (refuses a bare one-liner — asks for one) and human approval of the requirements draft before generating slices. The planning layer between an idea and ai-sdd-run. Use when starting a new feature in a repo that already has a .ai-sdd/ (run ai-sdd-bootstrap first). - **ai-sdd-run**: Drive a software-factory run by dispatching a sub-agent per worker — the deterministic `ai-sdd` engine plans (next), a fresh sub-agent does each worker's work via its skill, the engine gates and advances (submit), and each completed slice is committed. Agent-agnostic (claude-code or codex). Use when asked to run, drive, continue, or advance a factory run / pipeline / orchestration, or when the user mentions `ai-sdd next` / `ai-sdd submit`. ([215eight/ai-sdd](https://github.com/215eight/ai-sdd/tree/HEAD/.ai-sdd/skills/ai-sdd-run) · 2 copies)
